# Skills · S · part 246

882 entries, spatial-superb-eval to spdiary-gemini. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spatial-superb-eval**: Evaluates self-supervised speech representation models on downstream tasks including speaker identification, phoneme recognition, automatic speech recognition, emotion recognition, and speech localisation. It specifically probes robustness to noise and reverberation by comparing performance under clean versus noisy/reverberant training and testing conditions. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Spatial SUPERB, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports ASR WER. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/spatial-superb-eval) · ★3)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-clustering-interpretation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-co-expression-network-analysis**: Use when analysing normalised spatial transcriptomics matrices (e.g. Visium GSE261545) for cell type-specific co-expression modules, or when WGCNA/hdWGCNA over-merges modules (grey/turquoise) and finer spatial module patterns are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-co-expression-network-analysis))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-colorization**: Use when visualizing cell-type clusters from single-cell-resolution SRT data (STARmap, Stereo-seq, seqFISH, IMC) where default palettes (Scanpy/matplotlib) cause perceptual ambiguity between spatially adjacent or interlaced cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-colorization))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-coordinate-processing**: Use when spot spatial coordinates (e.g. simu_st_metadata.csv from the Figshare DOI 10.6084/m9.figshare.24452812 deposit) are available and an N x N ground-truth adjacency target is needed for STEM's spatial-relationship reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-coordinate-processing))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when preparing a raw spatial multi-omics count matrix (mRNA + protein modalities, e.g., spatial-CITE-seq or Stereo-CITE-seq) as input for spatial dimension reduction such as SMOPCA, and gene-level feature selection is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-count-matrix-preprocessing))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-data-analysis**: Use when paired spatial-transcriptomics and scRNA-seq datasets (e.g. MDA-MB-231-GFP xenograft spatial spots plus Chromium 10X scRNA-seq) must be integrated to infer spatial patterns or cross-domain gene correlations at single-cell resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-data-analysis))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-data-handling**: Use when you have a poly(A) site-by-spot matrix plus a gene-spot expression matrix and spot coordinates from spatially barcoded ST data, and you need APA usage quantification, KNN imputation, spot clustering, or imputation benchmarking against scDaPars. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-data-handling))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-data-processing**: Use when spatial transcriptomics data (expression matrix with spot coordinates) must be processed to detect and localize TME spatial structures such as TLSs or tumor capsules using gene-signature spatial autocorrelation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-data-processing))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-data-simulation**: Use when a spatial transcriptomics method (e.g., stImage integrative strategies) must be benchmarked against known ground truth, since real ST data lack definitive per-spot cluster labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-data-simulation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-dataset-subsampling**: Use when benchmarking computational scalability of an SRT method — e.g. verifying a claim that runtime scales linearly with the number of spatial locations on datasets with thousands or more spots or beads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-dataset-subsampling))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-deconvolution-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare ST deconvolution methods on datasets with known ground-truth cell type proportions, or measure runtime and peak RAM of SpatialDDLS vs RCTD vs cell2location on identical inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-deconvolution-benchmarking))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-deconvolution-cell2location**: Visium and other spot-based spatial transcriptomics (Slide-seq, ST) cell type deconvolution with cell2location: load Space Ranger output, get a tissue-matched scRNA-seq reference from CELLxGENE, learn cell type signatures, estimate cell type abundance per spot with posterior uncertainty, and check the maps against marker genes. Default route for 'annotate cell types' on Visium when a GPU is available. Limits: needs a tissue- and state-matched reference; the default 30000 epochs take hours on CPU, so without a GPU it runs only with fewer epochs (exploratory maps) and can exceed one execution c… ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-transcriptomics-deconvolution-cell2location) · ★394 · 3 copies)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-differential-activity-detection**: Use when you have a spatial transcriptomics dataset (e.g., 10x Visium) with an expression matrix and 2D spatial coordinates, and need to rank spatially variable genes cluster-independently or compare rankings across SVG methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-differential-activity-detection))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-domain-clustering**: Use when integrating and spatially clustering multiple SRT sections (e.g., 12-sample human DLPFC 10x Visium) into known domain counts, or when benchmarking clustering accuracy (ARI/NMI) against methods like BASS, SC.MEB, BayesSpace, SpaGCN, Louvain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-domain-clustering))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-embedding-integration**: Use when you already hold per-spot embeddings from multiple spatial transcriptomics modalities (expression, spatial adjacency, histology image) sharing the same n_spots dimension and need a unified attention-weighted embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-embedding-integration))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-expression-normalization**: Use when analyzing Space Ranger count matrices from 10X Visium spots (e.g., 747 day-4 chicken heart spots from 5 hearts, 1995 day-14 spots) where spatial uTAR expression must be compared on a common log scale with gene markers such as TNNT2, COL1A1, or HBZ. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-expression-normalization))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-feature-filtering**: Use when constructing a SpatialDDLS object from paired scRNA-seq (SingleCellExperiment) and spatial transcriptomics (SpatialExperiment) data and you must define the feature set via logFC-based per-cluster filtering before simulating mixed transcriptional profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-feature-filtering))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-figure-reproduction**: Use when validating cell-cell communication predictions against spatial transcriptomics data — e.g., correlating LIANA-ranked LR interactions with spot-level cell-type colocalization (Visium/SPOTlight) or neighborhood enrichment (seqFISH/merFISH). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-figure-reproduction))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-gene-count-aggregation**: Use when a cell segmentation (vendor or Baysor/Cellpose) already exists for MERSCOPE/Xenium transcripts and you need a cell-by-gene count matrix, e.g. prior to clustering, UMAP, or Calinski-Harabasz comparison of segmentations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-gene-count-aggregation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-gene-detection-metrics**: Use when comparing downstream annotation quality (CellTypist confidence) and per-cell gene detection between 2 μm-bin-derived reconstructed cells and default 8 μm bins from Visium HD data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-gene-detection-metrics))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-gene-filtering**: Use when preparing spot-level count matrices (e.g. a SpatialExperiment with raw counts) for SVG detection or spatial clustering, where low-expression and mitochondrial genes must be removed to reduce runtime and improve p-value calibration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-gene-filtering))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-gene-imputation**: Use when a low-plex spatial assay (e.g., 153-gene MERFISH hypothalamus data) needs prediction of unmeasured genes from a matched scRNA-seq reference across random training/test gene splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-gene-imputation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-gene-ranking-interpretation**: Use when you have per-gene SVG rankings (e.g., LR_stat and rank columns from nnSVG's rowData output) and need to interpret them, select top-ranked or significant genes, or assess agreement between methods' rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-gene-ranking-interpretation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-gpu**: Cell segmentation, transcript assignment, BIDCell, segger, PanoSpace, and foundation models for spatial omics. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/spatial-transcriptomics-gpu) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-image-analysis**: Process and analyze tissue images from spatial transcriptomics data using Squidpy. Extract image features, segment cells/nuclei, and compute morphological features from H&E or IF images. Use when processing tissue images for spatial transcriptomics. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/image-analysis) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-image-segmentation**: Use when raw spatial transcriptomics imaging data (e.g. RESOLVE, MERSCOPE, Xenium, Visium HD image layers in a SpatialData object) must be converted into per-cell segmentation masks before transcript allocation or downstream clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-image-segmentation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-integration**: Use when you have paired spatial transcriptomics and spatial metabolomics datasets from the same sample(s) that are at different spatial resolutions or coordinate systems, and you need to integrate them for joint analysis of cross-modal spatial patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-integration) · 2 copies)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-malignant-region-identification**: Use when analyzing tumor ST spot-level data to locate malignant regions against pathologist annotations, especially when comparing spot-region calls to benchmarks such as Cancer-Finder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-malignant-region-identification))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-malignant-spot-annotation**: Use when you have 10x Visium spot-level count matrices (slides with suspected malignant regions) and need reference-free malignant/non-malignant spot calls without a reference or spatial deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-malignant-spot-annotation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-mapper**: Map spatial transcriptomics data from 10x Genomics Visium/Xenium onto. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Data%20Analysis/spatial-transcriptomics-mapper) · ★2.1k)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-matrix-processing**: Use when working with 10X Visium HD 2 μm bin count matrices (AnnData/h5ad) that show a striped pattern in per-bin count totals, or when preparing bins for downstream cell reconstruction and neighbourhood analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-matrix-processing))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-network-inference**: Use when building interpretable GRNs from whole-brain Visium or other spatial transcriptomics count matrices (e.g. mouse AD plaque/age data) and scoring network modules per spot for spatial or disease association. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-network-inference))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-niche-analysis-workflow**: Use when image-based spatial transcriptomics includes transcript coordinates, staining channels and an aligned histology image, and needs resolved cell boundaries, per-cell measurements and image-derived spatial niches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/workflows/spatial-transcriptomics-niche-analysis))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-pathway-activity-scoring**: Use when analyzing Visium spatial transcriptomics data to identify biological pathways (gene sets) whose per-spot activity levels are non-randomly distributed across tissue coordinates, rather than testing individual genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-pathway-activity-scoring))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw Seq-Scope 1st-seq/2nd-seq FASTQ files (spatial barcodes in Illumina sequence identifiers plus cDNA reads) must be converted into DGE matrices, Seurat objects, clustering, and subcellular plots at ultra-high spatial resolution (&gt;1M barcodes/mm2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-pipeline-execution))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-preprocessing**: Use when raw ST count matrices (10x Visium, MERSCOPE, MERFISH, Xenium) must be normalized and filtered before training CeLEry for 2D location or spatial domain recovery, or before VAE-based data augmentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-preprocessing))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-proteomics-integration**: Use when you have paired spatial transcriptomics and proteomics data in AnnData (h5ad) format with coordinates in obsm['spatial'] and need clusters that respect spatial tissue structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-proteomics-integration))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-reconstruction**: Use when reconstructing 2D spatial gene expression maps from single-cell expression data (e.g., mouse cerebellum slide-seq, 46,293 locations) without marker genes, and when benchmarking reconstruction accuracy vs number of CCSF components. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-reconstruction))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-reference-preprocessing**: Use when reconstructing pseudo-space from scRNA-seq with scSpace and the candidate spatial reference (Visium/STARmap/Slide-seq) may differ architecturally from the target tissue, e.g., DLPFC vs agranular dACC lacking cortical layer 4. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-reference-preprocessing))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-regulome-scoring**: Use when TF expression is too sparse in spatial transcriptomics spots (e.g. Kuppe et al. 2022 myocardial infarction data) to map TF activity directly, and an inferred GRN edge list of TF-target links is available to build target-gene regulome scores per spot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-regulome-scoring))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-rexel-parcellation**: Use when you have spatial scRNAseq data (e.g., MERFISH cell coordinates + log-normalized cell-by-gene matrix) and need to segment tissue into spatial regions by comparing neighborhoods as transcriptome distributions rather than single cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-rexel-parcellation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-rgb-visualization**: Use when you have a digital expression matrix with spatial coordinates (e.g., Seq-Scope Velocyto-format DGE from STARsolo alignment) and need to visualize marker gene sets or subcellular spliced/unspliced read patterns at sub-micrometer resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-rgb-visualization))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-scrnaseq-mapping**: Use when separately measured single-cell domains (spatial RNA-seq and scRNA-seq, e.g. Chromium 10X from tumor xenografts) must be aligned so that mapped points retain per-sample correspondence to their inputs, not just distribution-level similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-scrnaseq-mapping))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-simulation**: Use when a benchmark study needs controlled spatial multi-omics data with ground-truth spatial domain labels, e.g., simulating 3-omics DLPFC sections to compare SMOPCA against SpatialPCA, SpaVAE, or SpatialGlue_3M. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-simulation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-simulation**: Use when validating a spatial-pathway or spatial-relevance method on ST data where no ground truth exists: simulate tissue patterns with SRTsim (or SERGIO-style scripts from the manuscript repo), plant known spatially relevant gene sets, and test whether the method separates them from random sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-simulation))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-simulation-framework**: Use when benchmarking or validating an SVG detection method's p-value calibration and sensitivity — e.g., measuring FPR at nominal 0.01/0.05/0.1 cutoffs, running coordinate-shuffle ablations, or building coordinate-permuted null simulations for spatially-resolved transcriptomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-simulation-framework))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-simulation-from-scrnaseq**: Use when you need ground-truth-controlled ST data to test whether a deconvolution method recovers known cell-type proportions or condition-specific gene-expression changes (e.g., age-associated macrophage Cd274/Clec4d up, Coro1a down) from pixel mixtures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-simulation-from-scrnaseq))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-communication**: Analyze cell-cell communication in spatial transcriptomics data using ligand-receptor analysis with Squidpy. Infer intercellular signaling, identify communication pathways, and visualize interaction networks. Use when analyzing cell-cell communication in spatial context. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-communication) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-data-io**: Load spatial transcriptomics data from Visium, Xenium, MERFISH, Slide-seq, and other platforms using Squidpy and SpatialData. Use this skill when: (1) Loading Visium spatial transcriptomics data from Space Ranger output, (2) Loading Xenium single-cell resolution spatial data, (3) Loading MERFISH, CosMx, or other spatial platforms, (4) Converting between SpatialData and AnnData formats. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-data-io) · ★1.1k)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-data-io**: Load spatial transcriptomics data from Visium, Xenium, MERFISH, Slide-seq, and other platforms using Squidpy and SpatialData. Read Space Ranger outputs, convert formats, and access spatial coordinates. Use when loading Visium, Xenium, MERFISH, or other spatial data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-data-io) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-deconvolution**: Estimate cell type composition in spatial transcriptomics spots using reference-based deconvolution. Use cell2location, RCTD, SPOTlight, or Tangram to infer cell type proportions from scRNA-seq references. Use when estimating cell type composition in spatial spots. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-deconvolution) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-domains**: Identify spatial domains and tissue regions in spatial transcriptomics data using Squidpy and Scanpy. Cluster spots considering both expression and spatial context to define anatomical regions. Use when identifying tissue domains or spatial regions. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-domains) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-multiomics**: Analyze high-resolution spatial platforms like Slide-seq, Stereo-seq, and Visium HD. Use when working with subcellular resolution or high-density spatial data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-multiomics) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-neighbors**: Build spatial neighbor graphs for spatial transcriptomics data using Squidpy. Compute k-nearest neighbors, Delaunay triangulation, and radius-based connectivity for downstream spatial analyses. Use when building spatial neighborhood graphs. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-neighbors) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-preprocessing**: Quality control, filtering, normalization, and feature selection for spatial transcriptomics data. Calculate QC metrics, filter spots/cells, normalize counts, and identify highly variable genes. Use when filtering and normalizing spatial transcriptomics data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-preprocessing) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-proteomics**: Analyzes spatial proteomics data from CODEX, IMC, and MIBI platforms including cell segmentation and protein colocalization. Use when working with multiplexed imaging data, analyzing protein spatial patterns, or integrating spatial proteomics with transcriptomics. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-proteomics) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-statistics**: Compute spatial statistics for spatial transcriptomics data using Squidpy. Calculate Moran's I, Geary's C, spatial autocorrelation, co-occurrence analysis, and neighborhood enrichment. Use when computing spatial autocorrelation or co-occurrence statistics. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-statistics) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spatial-visualization**: Visualize spatial transcriptomics data using Squidpy and Scanpy. Create tissue plots with gene expression, clusters, and annotations overlaid on histology images. Use when visualizing spatial expression patterns. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/spatial-transcriptomics/spatial-visualization) · ★1)
- **spatial-transcriptomics-splicing-concordance**: Use when validating that exon-level splicing changes (delta-PSI) found between brain regions in single-cell ScISOrSeq data reproduce in spatial transcriptomics data with ONT long-read alignments, e.g. HIPP vs PFC on a defined exon set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-splicing-concordance))
- **spatial-transcriptomics-spot-clustering**: Use when you have a spot-level ratio matrix (e.g., RUD APA usage from stAPAminer) plus spot coordinates from spatially barcoded ST data (e.g., MOB) and need to cluster spots, embed them, and validate clusters against tissue layer annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-transcriptomics-spot-clustering))

- **spdd**: Structured Prompt-Driven Development по Мартину Фаулеру — методология разработки новой системы через управляемые промпты как артефакты уровня кода. Используй ВСЕГДА, когда пользователь хочет создать с нуля новую систему/приложение/сервис/продукт/MVP/модуль/фичу — даже при размытом запросе ("давай сделаем X", "хочу создать Y", "придумаем сервис для Z", "нужна система для", "спроектируем", "начнём новый проект", "построим", "разработаем"). Скилл проводит через 6 шагов: декомпозиция требований → анализ → контекст → структурированный промпт REASONS → генерация кода → юнит-тесты.
